Let's focus on a powerful local search: **top-shift coordinate minimization via 1D Fourier active sets**.

Exact active set approach:
At h, compute corr, find max value M. Let active indices where corr ≥ M - tol. Consider minimizing max of these active functions while ensuring they don't exceed M - reduction. We can solve a **quadratic program**: minimize λ + small regularization s.t. F_k(h+d)≤λ, ||d||≤ρ, sum d=0, bounds. But F_k quadratic nonconvex. We can linearize F_k around h: F_k ≈ F_k0 + g_k·d (ignore quadratic). Then solve least-norm steepest descent direction to reduce active correlations: minimize ||d||² s.t. g_k·d ≤ -η for active k, sum d=0, bounds step. This can be solved via QP or approximate. The previous direction g2-g1 is special. We can use active weighted gradient: d = -∑w g_k. But to equalize, if gradients not parallel, weighted can reduce top. Use weights=1/top count.

Could implement **Frank-Wolfe / mirror descent** for the dual of smooth max: For softmax, the gradient already does this. So SLSQP/softmax is good.

Maybe use **Nelder-Mead on low-dimensional Fourier coefficients** as a complementary global search. Could find a different construction with lower C5. Let's explore Fourier param more.

Represent h length n by discrete cosine transform coefficients with zero mean and bounded values. We can optimize coefficients a (real vector length n) with constraint 0≤0.5+0.5u≤1 i.e. u∈[-1,1]. Let u be DCT/FFT real vector? If we parameterize u = 2tanh(z) to ensure bounds, optimize z coefficients? Sum constraint: need mean u=0 (so h mean 0.5). We can enforce by setting zero-frequency coefficient 0 and maybe projecting. For low-dimensional z (first M Fourier modes), h = 0.5 + 0.5*tanh(z(x)) maybe not exactly zero mean; project to fix sum, but that changes z.

Better: use **truncated Fourier series on grid**: u_i = ∑_{m=1}^M (a_m cos(2π m i/n)+b_m sin(...)) maybe cyclic. But our finite interval not periodic; still any grid vector can be represented. Detailed functions:

`project_feasible(v, n)`:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    target=n/2.0
    # binary search lambda
    lo=np.min(v)-1
    hi=np.max(v)+1
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       h=np.clip(v-mid,0,1)
       s=np.sum(h)
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust residual tiny by adding to interior
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       mask=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/np.sum(mask)
       else: h+=diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # final water-fill again maybe
    return h
```
This finds λ. But `v` may already feasible; binary search 50* n loops can be costly. We can first check if min≥0 max≤1 and sum close; if feasible return. If only sum off, adjust. For speed, use sorting algorithm O(n log n) maybe. Binary 50*n for n=320=16k operations per projection; if many (line search thousands) =16M, okay. For n=1024=51k, 1000=51M, okay. Could optimize with exact algorithm:
Sort v ascending. For target T between 0 and n. We need λ. For a given λ, sum = ∑_{v_i>λ} (v_i-λ) clipped 1. We can find interval. Simpler binary is fine. Reduce iterations to 40. Check tolerance 1e-12.

def smooth_objective(x):
   corr=corr_full(x)
   m=np.max(corr)
   beta=_BETA
   shifted=beta*(corr-m)
   ex=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,700))
   s=ex.sum()
   return (m + np.log(s)/beta)*(2.0/_N)
```

Thus gradient function:
```
def softmax_grad(x):
 n=_N; beta=_BETA
 corr=corr_full(x)
 m=np.max(corr)
 cl=np.clip(beta*(corr-m), -700, 700)
 ex=np.exp(cl); w=ex/ex.sum()
 g=np.zeros(n)
 W0=w[n-1]
 if W0>0: g += W0*(1-2*x)
 for q in range(1,n):
    Wp=w[n-1+q]
    Wm=w[n-1-q]
    s=Wp+Wm
    a=n-q
    if s > 1e-15:
       g[:a] += Wm - s*x[q:a+q] # x[q:q+a]
       g[q:] += Wp - s*x[:a] # x[0:a]
 # Wait for i≥q, h_{i-q}=x[0:a], weight Wp - s? yes
 return g*(2.0/n)
```
Check slices: `x[q:a+q]` length a, indices q..n-1. `x[:a]` length a.

Let's think of **continuous coordinate descent with exact single variable minimization**. Given sum constraint, adjusting one variable changes sum; but can adjust another. Pair moves. We can implement more robust continuous pair search with gradient direction:
- Compute soft gradient g (scaled). Let p = argmin g (increase), q=argmax g (decrease). If g[p] < g[q], move from q to p: h[p]+=δ, h[q]-=δ.
- Line search over δ (positive) using feasible limits. Evaluate exact c5; accept best. This is steepest coordinate ascent for sum-preserving. Repeat until no improvement. This can be fast and maintain sum exactly. It might reduce C5. If no pair improvement, try next top pairs (top 5 negatives/positives). This is like Frank-Wolfe.
- Use exact c5 each trial; n=320, pair line search many. We can vectorize deltas? Just full correlate.

def binary_local_search(h,n, time_limit, rng):
    # threshold and fix count
    h = np.zeros(n); choose n/2 ones
    corr = compute_corr(h); cur = max; best=cur
    start=time.time()
    stall=0
    while time - start < time_limit and remaining()>5:
       idx = int(np.argmax(corr)); q=idx-(n-1)
       ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       # candidate sets for top q

          bad_ones = ones[(ones+q<n)] & (h[ones+q]==0)
       else:
          bad_ones = ones[(ones>=-q)] & (h[ones-q]==0)? Check q<0: F=∑ h_m(1-h_{m-q}), m-q valid m+|q| <n. So m < n+q. bad if h[m+|q|]==0.

             q<0, p in [-q,n-1] and h[p+q]==1 (p-q? Actually q=-r, m? If increase zero at p, it can reduce term m=p+r? Need h[m]=1, m = p - q? For q=-r, term h_m(1-h_{m+r}); if p=m+r, need h_m=h[p-r]=1. So p-r in range h[p-r]=1.)
       Use randomized candidate i from bad_ones if any else ones, j from good_zeros if any else zeros.
       For t in range(ntry):
           i... j...
           h[i]=0;h[j]=1; newcorr=compute_corr(h); newmax=...
           if newmax < cur - 1e-12 or (accept worse with prob): accept
       if stall > ... random perturbation
    return h,best

So delta vectorized:
```
def swap_delta(h,i,j, q):
    L=len(q); n=len(h)
    # T1 valid_m for p:
    lo = np.maximum(0, q)
    hi = np.minimum(n-1, n-1+q)
    d = np.zeros(L)
    d[(i>=lo)&(i<=hi)] -= 1
    d[(j>=lo)&(j<=hi)] += 1
    # T2
    dest_i = i + q
    mask = (dest_i>=0)&(dest_i<n)
    d[mask] += h[dest_i[mask]]
    dest_j = j + q
    mask = (dest_j>=0)&(dest_j<n)
    d[mask] -= h[dest_j[mask]]
    # T3
    dest_i = i - q
    mask = ...
    d[mask] += h[dest_i[mask]]
    dest_j = j - q
    mask ... d[mask] -= h[dest_j[mask]]
    # T4
    d[0? q=0 index n-1] -= 2
    diff = i - j
    idx1 = n-1 + diff
    idx2 = n-1 - diff
    if 0<=idx1<L: d[idx1] += 1
    if 0<=idx2<L: d[idx2] += 1
    return d
```
For q=0, the cross ±d terms not at 0 unless i=j; add cross separately, subtract 2 at center. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def apply_pair_delta(h, i, j, a, b):
    n=len(h); q=_Q[n]? We'll pass q.
    d = np.zeros(len(q))
    # T1/T3 for i
    mi = (q >= i - n + 1) & (q <= i)
    if np.any(mi):
        d[mi] += a - a * h[i - q[mi]]  # T1 + a, T3 -a*h_{i-q}
    # T2 for i
    pi = (q >= -i) & (q < n - i)
    if np.any(pi): d[pi] += -a * h[i + q[pi]]
    # repeat j
    mj=(q >= j-n+1)&(q <= j); d[mj] += b - b*h[j - q[mj]]
    pj=(q >= -j)&(q < n-j); d[pj] += -b*h[j + q[pj]]
    # T4
    c0 = n-1
    d[c0] -= a*a + b*b  # q=0 self terms
    diff = i - j
    idx1 = n-1 + diff
    idx2 = n-1 - diff
    if 0<=idx1< L:
       d[idx1] += -a*b
    if 0<=idx2< L:
       d[idx2] += -a*b
    return d
```

However using delta requires maintaining `corr` and after accept update. Need if recompute `corr` after some operations, drift. We can recompute from h every e.g. 1000 accepted moves or after perturb.

**Continuous pair search using delta** algorithm more concretely:
```
def pair_descent(h, n, time_limit, rng):
    global q arrays? 
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur_max = float(corr.max())
    best = cur_max * 2/n
    q_arr = np.arange(2*n-1) - (n-1)
    start=time.time()
    comm = 0
    while time_left() > 5 and time.time()-start < time_limit:
        if comm > 2000:
            corr = compute_corr(h); comm=0
        # top indices
        top_idxs = np.argsort(-corr)[:K] maybe K=3
        # compute weighted gradient from active shifts (weights softmax with beta maybe 100 over top? or uniform)
        g = zeros(n)

        best_improve=0
        # try pairs (i in high, p in low) maybe 16 pairs
        for i in high[:K2]:
          for p in low[:K2]:
             if i==p: continue
             a = -max_step? To decrease i, increase p. Feasible maxδ = min(h[i], 1-h[p])
             if maxδ<=0: continue
             # Try δ fractions [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1]
             for frac in fracs:
                 δ=frac*maxδ
                 if δ<=1e-8: continue
                 d=pair_delta(h,i,p,-δ,δ,...)
                 newmax = float(np.max(corr+d))
                 if newmax < cur_max - 1e-12:
                     accept best
             if accepted: update h, corr, cur_max=best_new, break loops
        if not improved:
           # try random pairs based on g or random
           for attempt...

              # perturb: add small Gaussian to h, project, recompute corr, cur_max=...
              # if perturb worsens but best preserved, continue from current best? Keep best_h separate.
    return best_h,best_c5
```

```
def grad_for_shift(h,n,kidx):
    q = kidx - (n-1)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if q>=0:
       m = n-q
       g[q:q+m] += 1 - h[:m]
       g[:m] -= h[q:q+m]
    else:
       r = -q
       m = n-r
       g[:m] += 1 - h[r:r+m]
       g[r:r+m] -= h[:m]
    return g
```
Check q>=0 mapping n=2 q=1: m=1, g[1]+=1-h0; g[0]-=h1: derivative [ -b,1-a] correct. q<0 r=1: m=1, g[0]+=1-h1; g[1]-=h0 correct.

```
tr = corr.copy()
L=2*n-1
# i contributions
mi = (q >= i - n + 1) & (q <= i)
if mi.any():
    dest = i - q[mi]
    tr[mi] += a - a * h[dest]
pi = (q >= -i) & (q < n - i)
if pi.any(): tr[pi] += -a * h[i + q[pi]]
# j similar
mj...
# self T4
tr[n-1] -= a*a + b*b
diff=i-j
idx1=n-1+diff; idx2=n-1-diff
if 0<=idx1<L: tr[idx1] += -a*b
if 0<=idx2<L: tr[idx2] += -a*b
return tr
```

Let's implement `random_pair_quench` using delta to be robust:
```
def random_pair_quench(h,n,time_limit,rng):
    # make binary
    h = to_binary(h,n,rng)
    corr=compute_corr(h); cur=corr.max(); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    stall=0; start=time.time()
    while time left:
       idx=int(np.argmax(corr)); q=idx-(n-1)
       ones = idx h==1; zeros h==0
       # candidate i,j based on q
       if q>=0:
          bad = ones[(ones+q<n)&(h[ones+q]==0)]
             good_zeros = zeros[(zeros+q<n)&(h[zeros+q]==1)]
       else: r=-q
          bad = ones[(ones+r<n)&(h[ones+r]==0)]
          good_zeros = zeros[(zeros>=r)&(h[zeros-r]==1)]
       try 100 random i from bad or ones, j from good_zeros or zeros
       for trial:
           a=-1.0,b=+1.0
           tr=pair_trial(h,corr,i,j,-1,1,q_arr,n)
           nm=tr.max()
           if nm < cur - 1e-12: accept (h[i]=0;h[j]=1;corr=tr;cur=nm;best update) break
       if no improve:
          # try random pairs from top based? maybe 20
          if stall>20: perturbation accept worse with prob
    return best
```
Because pair_trial O(n), 100 trials*1000=100k*640 ops=64M, fine.

Need `pair_trial` uses h before swap. If accept, h updates and corr=tr. For binary swap, if h[i]=1 exactly and h[j]=0, after update exact.

**Candidate selection in binary quench**: Need ones/zeros arrays; if using h==1 float, after pair updates exact values? We update h[i]=0.0,h[j]=1.0; good.
For q mapping: corr index k = n-1+q. F_q =∑ h_m(1-h_{m-q}) with range. Bad ones (as m) where term=1: h_m=1 and h_{m-q}=0.
For q>=0: m from q..n-1; condition m-q <n automatically; m-q valid. bad ones: i (as m) with h_i=1, i-q in [0,n-1] (i>=q) and h_{i-q}=0. So `ones[(ones>=q) & (h[ones-q]==0)]` (ones-q nonnegative). For q<0: r=-q, m from 0..n-r-1; m+ r valid. bad ones: h_i=1, i+r<n, h[i+r]=0. `ones[(ones+r<n)&(h[ones+r]==0)]`.
Good zeros to increase: changing j from 0 to1 decreases F_q if j is the second index m-q for a bad term, i.e., h_{j+q}=1 and valid. For q>=0: j+q valid j<n-q and h[j+q]=1. Also changing zero to one can increase if j as first with h_{j-q}=0; we want helpful zeros where it decreases. So `zeros[(j+q<n)&(h[j+q]==1)]`. For q<0: m+r = p => p=r..n-1; h[p-r]=1. So `zeros[(j>=r)&(h[j-r]==1)]`.
If bad or good empty, use all ones/zeros.

Now **project_feasible water-filling**: Ensure robust. Implementation details:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    if v.size != n: ...
    if np.min(v)>=-1e-12 and np.max(v)<=1+1e-12 and abs(np.sum(v)-target)<=1e-9:
        v=np.clip(v,0,1)
        # adjust small diff in interior
        diff=target-np.sum(v)
        if abs(diff)>1e-12:
           m=(v>1e-12)&(v<1-1e-12)
           if m.any(): v[m]+=diff/np.sum(m)
           else: v+=diff/n
           v=np.clip(v,0,1)
        return v
    # water fill
    lo = min(np.min(v), target/n) - 1.0 # lambda lower? We need find λ.
    hi = max(np.max(v), target/n) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       h=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0)
       s=h.sum()
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-10:
       mask=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)
       else: h[:] += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # if still off, water fill again
       # Project exactly using deterministic assignment to last interior
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
The binary search variable λ: h=clip(v-λ,0,1). As λ increases, sum decreases. If s>target, need larger λ (lo=mid), else smaller (hi=mid). Initial lo,hi: if v values [0,1], λ in [min(v)-1, max(v)+1]. Good.
After small adjustment, sum may clip? Use a few iterations. For exact sum, we can do:
```
def force_sum(h,n):
    target=n/2
    for _ in range(5):
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       idx=np.argmax(h*(1-h))
       if 0<=h[idx]+diff<=1:
          h[idx]+=diff
       else:
          # fallback water fill again
          h=water_fill(h,n)
    return h
```
But adding diff to one changes sum to target as floating. We'll implement `project_feasible` with water fill and one-index adjust.

Water fill alternative exact via sorting maybe not needed.

Binary quench algorithm details:
```
def binary_quench(h0,n,time_limit,rng):
    h = make_binary(h0,n,rng)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur = float(corr.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
    q = np.arange(2*n-1)-(n-1)
    start=time.time(); stall=0; accepts=0
    while remaining()>5 and time.time()-start<time_limit:
       idx=int(np.argmax(corr)); qq=idx-(n-1)
       ones=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]
       # candidate arrays
       if qq>=0:
          bad = ones[(ones>=qq)&(h[ones-qq]==0)]
          good = zeros[(zeros< n-qq)&(h[zeros+qq]==1)]
       else:
          r=-qq
          bad = ones[(ones < n-r)&(h[ones+r]==0)]
          good = zeros[(zeros>=r)&(h[zeros-r]==1)]
       # if bad empty, use ones; if bad small, can also include ones random
       for _ in range(80):
          if len(bad)>0: i=bad[rng.randint(len(bad))] if rng.rand()<0.85 else ones[...]
          else: i=ones[...]
          if len(good)>0: j=good[rng.randint(len(good))] if rng.rand()<0.85 else zeros[...]
          else: j=zeros[...]
          if i==j: continue
          # h[i] should be 1, h[j] 0; if not due candidate? ensure
          if h[i]<0.5 or h[j]>0.5: continue
          tr=pair_trial(h,corr,i,j,-1.0,1.0,q,n)
          nm=float(tr.max())
          if nm < cur - 1e-9:
              h[i]=0;h[j]=1; corr=tr; cur=nm; accepts=0
              if cur<best_cur: best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
              break
       else:
          # random pair from all maybe
          improved=False
          for _ in range(20): random i,j; if nm<cur accept
          if not improved:
             stall+=1
             if stall>15:
                # perturbation: accept random swap even if worse maybe with limit
                # pick random i,j; h swap; corr=pair_trial; cur=...; stall=0
                # but don't update best if worse.
       if accepts>1000: recompute
    return best_h, best_cur*2/n
```
Need `pair_trial` for binary a=-1,b=1. If `_PAIR_DELTA_OK` false, it uses full h2; but h2 with binary. Good.

Simulated annealing: Instead of always revert to best, sometimes accept a worse swap to escape. If cur_max is integer, we can allow `nm <= cur + 1` with probability decreasing. But binary C5 quantized; local search can get stuck. Implement: if no improvement, after stall, accept a random swap if `nm - cur <= 1` or with probability 0.1. But this may wander. We can maintain a `temperature` starting based on best-cur. Simpler: after stall, do a random swap regardless (even if worse) to escape; if it worsens too much (e.g., cur-bad>5), revert. Since best saved. This may help but could waste.

Given continuous pair_descent likely enough, binary quench can be modest.

**`_pair_trial_delta` details** with q array. Need be efficient and correct. Let's code:
```
def _pair_trial_delta(h, corr, i, j, a, b, q, n):
    tr = corr.copy()
    Lq = len(q)
    center = n - 1
    # For index p with coeff c:
    # mask_valid_mq = (q >= p - n + 1) & (q <= p)
    mi = (q >= i - n + 1) & (q <= i)
    if mi.any():
        dest = i - q[mi]
        tr[mi] += a - a * h[dest]
    pi = (q >= -i) & (q < n - i)
    if pi.any():
        tr[pi] += -a * h[i + q[pi]]
    mj = (q >= j - n + 1) & (q <= j)
    if mj.any():
        dest = j - q[mj]
        tr[mj] += b - b * h[dest]
    pj = (q >= -j) & (q < n - j)
    if pj.any():
        tr[pj] += -b * h[j + q[pj]]
    # self
    tr[center] -= a*a + b*b
    diff = i - j
    idx1 = center + diff
    idx2 = center - diff
    if 0 <= idx1 < len(q): tr[idx1] += -a*b
    if 0 <= idx2 < len(q): tr[idx2] += -a*b
    return tr
```
Need if i+q array in pi: `i + q[pi]` gives int array; as src values, ensure dest int. q dtype int. Good.
T4 cross: If idx1==center? Only if diff=0 (not). If idx1==idx2? only diff=0. If diff nonzero, two distinct. If both same as center? no. Good.
But self T4 at center: For q=0, there are self terms -a^2 -b^2. Are cross terms also at center if i=j? skip. Good.

Let's test binary n=2 i=0,j=1,a=-1,b=1 q[-1,0,1]. `_pair_trial_delta` with h [1,0], corr [1,0,0].
center=1.
i mi q>=-1 & q<=0 -> true for -1,0. dest i-q: for q -1 =>1, q0=>0. tr += a - a*h: q-1: -1 -(-1*0)= -1; q0: -1 -(-1*1)=0. pi i: q>=0&q<2 -> q0,1? q<2 both 0,1. h[i+q]: q0 h0=1, q1 h1=0. tr += -a*h = +h: q0 +1, q1 0.
j mj: j=1, q>=0&q<=1 -> q0,1. dest j-q: q0=1, q1=0. tr += b -b*h: q0 1-0=1; q1 1-1=0.
pj j: q>=-1 & q<1 (n-j=1) -> q -1,0. dest j+q: -1=>0, 0=>1. tr += -b*h = -h: q-1 -1, q0 0.
T4 center q0 -=2; idx1 center+(-1)=0 +=1; idx2 center-(-1)=2 +=1.
Totals: q-1: -1 (mi) +(-1 pj)= -2 +1 = -1 correct. q0: i mi0 + pi1 + j mj1 + pj0 -2 =0 correct. q1: i pi0 + j mj0 +1=1 correct. Good.

For continuous a=-0.2,b=0.2, formula linear/quadratic okay.

**Potential bug**: `tr[mi] += a - a*h[dest]` where dest array. If a negative, okay. For p terms, T1+T3 combine. Good.

`weighted_active_grad(h,n,corr,beta=20,top_count=5)`:
```
top = np.argsort(-corr)[:top_count]
weights = np.exp(beta*(corr[top] - corr[top[0]])); weights/=weights.sum()
g=zeros(n)
for w,k in zip(weights,top):
   g += w*shift_grad(h,n,int(k))
return g
```
No lambda. zip okay.

**`pair_descent` implementation details**:
```
def pair_descent(h0, n, time_limit, rng=None):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(1)
   h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
   corr=compute_corr(h)
   cur=float(corr.max())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
   q=np.arange(2*n-1)-(n-1)
   start=time.time(); noimprove=0; acc=0; pert_level=0
   dt = 0.2*budget? no
   while remaining()>10 and time.time()-start<time_limit:
       # use current best? If current too far from best, maybe restart from best? We'll keep current.
       if acc > 300:
           corr=compute_corr(h); cur=corr.max(); acc=0
       g=weighted_active_grad(h,n,corr,beta=20,top_count=5)
       ordered=np.argsort(g)
       lows=ordered[:8]; highs=ordered[-8:]
       # ensure unique no overlap simple
       low_list=[]; for val in lows: if not in high_set add
       high_list=[] similarly.
       improved=False
       # try pairs
       for ii in high_list:
         for pp in low_list:
           if ii==pp: continue
           # direction: decrease ii, increase pp. If h[ii] <= h[pp]? maybe no.
           maxd = min(h[ii], 1.0 - h[pp])
           if maxd <= 1e-10: continue
           # If gradient direction wrong? Usually g[ii]>g[pp].
           if g[ii] - g[pp] < 1e-12: continue
           fracs=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8]
           best_trial=None; best_val=cur
           for f in fracs+ [1.0]:
              d=f*maxd
              if d<=1e-10: continue
              # if f==1, d = maxd*(1-1e-? use maxd)
              tr=pair_trial(h,corr,ii,pp,-d,d,q,n)
              nm=float(tr.max())
              if nm < best_val - 1e-11:
                   best_val=nm; best_trial=tr; best_d=d
           if best_trial is not None:
              h[ii]-=best_d; h[pp]+=best_d
              # clip small
              h[ii]=max(0.0,min(1.0,h[ii])); h[pp]=max(0.0,min(1.0,h[pp]))
              corr=best_trial
              cur=best_val
              acc += 1
              improved=True
              if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                  best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
              break
         if improved: break
       if improved:
           noimprove=0
           continue
       # no pair improvement: try random pairs using current gradient or random
       improved=False
       for _ in range(20):
          # if gradient has spread, random low/high
          ii=int(ordered[rng.randint(len(ordered))]); pp=...
          # maybe use top/bottom random
          ...
       if improved: noimprove=0; continue
       noimprove +=1
       if noimprove > 40:
           # perturb current from best? Use a perturbation of best_h (or current)
           sigma = [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1][min(pert_level,4)]
           h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,size=n), n) # start from best to avoid accumulating drift
           corr = compute_corr(h); cur=float(corr.max())
           pert_level +=1
           noimprove=0
           acc=0
   # final project best
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
   final=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   return best_h, final
```
Need be careful: If we start perturb from `best_h`, but current best_h may have been improved; good. We discard current local. This is basin hopping. But if best_h is at a local min and perturbation small, pair_descent may re-converge to same. Sigma increases eventually. We can also perturb current instead of best maybe. Starting from best safer.
`pert_level` resets when improved beyond previous? Maybe not. If we keep perturbing best with larger sigmas, may find new basin. But if we move away from best, need budget. Good.
The `best_h` return is best over all; pair_descent may spend after finding target. Time limit ensures.

Potential issue: In pair line search, we use `pair_trial` which returns a full `corr` array. Storing `best_trial` for each pair; if many, copies. We can reduce by accepting first improvement rather than best fraction? To be safe, try fractions and keep best. But copies for each fraction. We can optimize by not storing all: if nm < cur, accept immediately? But a small fraction might improve, larger maybe better. Accepting first found could lead to slow tiny steps. We can use a linear search: try increasingly larger fractions and accept last that improves? Actually if δ=0.02 improves, δ=0.05 might be worse; the function not monotonic. We want best among safe. Copying 8 arrays length 640 is small. okay.

def slsqp_betas(h,n,betas,maxiter_per,budget_fraction):
   best=h; best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   for beta in betas:
       if remaining()<20: break
       h2=slsqp_refine(best,n,beta,maxiter_per)
       c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c: ...
   return best,best_c
```

**SLSQP gradient full** uses our slice formula. Let's implement `full_soft_grad(x)` correctly:
```
def full_soft_grad(x):
   n=_G_N; beta=_G_BETA
   corr=compute_corr(x)
   m=corr.max()
   shifted=np.clip(beta*(corr-m), -700,700)
   ex=np.exp(shifted)
   w=ex/ex.sum()
   g=np.zeros(n)
   W0=w[n-1]
   if W0>0: g += W0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
   # For q=1..n-1:
   # Wp = w[n-1+q] offset +q (h_m(1-h_{m-q}))
   # Wm = w[n-1-q] offset -q
   for q in 1..n-1:
      Wp=w[n-1+q]; Wm=w[n-1-q]; s=Wp+Wm
      if s <= 1e-15: continue
      a=n-q
      # offset +q: g[i] for first factor? Use formula derived for corr offset q: g_i = for q: Wq [1_{i-q valid}(1-h_{i-q}) - 1_{i+q valid} h_{i+q}]
      # Combined:
      # i<n-q (i+q valid): Wm - s*h[i+q] ? Let's rederive using corr q definition.
```
We earlier derived for corr offset q (positive q corresponds h_i, h_{i-q} with weight Wp; negative offset -q weight Wm). combine:
For p=i<n-q (i+q valid): contribution involving h_{i+q}:
- For Wm (offset -q), first factor? offset -q: derivative at i as first if i+q valid: +Wm(1-h_{i+q}).
- For Wp (offset +q), second factor? offset +q: derivative at i as second if i-q? Wait offset +q derivative at index i: first if i-q valid: +Wp(1-h_{i-q}); second if i+q valid: -Wp h_{i+q}. So for i<n-q, from Wp second: -Wp h_{i+q}; from Wm first: Wm(1-h_{i+q}). Total Wm - (Wp+Wm)h_{i+q}. Good.
For i>=q (i-q valid): from Wp first: +Wp(1-h_{i-q}); from Wm second: -Wm h_{i-q}. Total Wp - (Wp+Wm)h_{i-q}. Good.
Thus slices:
```
g[:a] += Wm - s * x[q:q+a]
g[q:] += Wp - s * x[:a]
```
This matches earlier corrected. Test q=1 n=2 Wp corr2 derivative [ -Wp*h1, Wp(1-h0) ]; Wm corr0 derivative [Wm(1-h1), -Wm*h0]. Combined: i<n-q=1: g0 += Wm - (Wp+Wm)h1; i>=1: g1 += Wp - (Wp+Wm)h0. For Wp only: g0=-h1, g1=1-h0 correct. For Wm only: Wp=0 -> g0=1-h1, g1=-h0 correct. Good.
```
return g*(2.0/n)
```

def slsqp_refine(h0,n,beta,maxiter=200):
   global ...
   h=np.array(h0,float)
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   _G_N=n; _G_BETA=float(beta); _G_TGT=n/2.0
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_fun,'jac':_sum_jac}]
   try:
       res=minimize(full_soft_obj, h, jac=full_soft_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'iprint':0})
       h2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       return h2
   except Exception:
       return h
```

def full_direction_step(h, g, q, n):
    d = -g.copy()
    d -= np.mean(d)
    # zero tiny? if all tiny return None,None
    pos = d > 0; neg = d < 0

def full_dir_improve(h,corrg,maxc,g,n):
   d=-g; d-=np.mean(d)
   pos=d>1e-12; neg=d<-1e-12
   if not (pos.any() or neg.any()): return None
   a=1e18
   if pos.any(): a=min(a, np.min((1-h[pos])/d[pos]))
   if neg.any(): a=min(a, np.min(h[neg]/(-d[neg])))
   if a<=1e-12: return None
   best_trial=None; best_val=maxc
   for f in [0.02,...,1.0]:
       aa=f*a*(1-1e-12)
       h2=np.clip(h+aa*d,0,1)
       h2=project_feasible(h2,n) # ensures sum
       c2=compute_c5? Need max corr: cor2=compute_corr(h2); v=cor2.max()
       if v<best_val-1e-11: best...
   return besth,bestcorr,bestval

Let's write `project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    if v.shape[0]!=n:
       # fallback
    target=n/2.0
    # quick check
    vmin=np.min(v); vmax=np.max(v)
    if vmin >= -1e-12 and vmax <= 1.0+1e-12:
       w=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
       diff=target - np.sum(w)
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-10:
           # make small exact adjust
           if abs(diff)>1e-13:
               idx=int(np.argmax(w*(1.0-w)))
               nv=w[idx]+diff
               if 0.0 <= nv <= 1.0:
                   w[idx]=nv
           return w
       # water fill
    lo = min(float(np.min(v)), target/float(n)) - 1.0
    hi = max(float(np.max(v)), target/float(n)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        w=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0)
        s=float(np.sum(w))
        if s > target:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    w=np.clip(v-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    # final adjust
    for _ in range(10):
        w=np.clip(w,0.0,1.0)
        diff=target - float(np.sum(w))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        idx=int(np.argmax(w*(1.0-w)))
        nv=w[idx]+diff
        if 0.0 <= nv <= 1.0:
            w[idx]=nv
        else:
            # distribute to interior
            mask=(w>1e-12)&(w<1.0-1e-12)
            if np.any(mask):
                w[mask] += diff/float(np.sum(mask))
            else:
                w += diff/float(n)
    return np.clip(w,0.0,1.0)
```
Potential infinite if diff big and no interior? If all 0/1 and target impossible? target average 0.5, there should be. Good.

`interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(src,dst):
    sn=len(src)
    if sn==dst: return src.copy()
    x=(np.arange(sn)+0.5)*2.0/sn
    yn=(np.arange(dst)+0.5)*2.0/dst
    return np.interp(yn,x,src)
```

`to_binary`:
```
def to_binary(h,n,rng):
    b=np.where(h>0.5,1.0,0.0)
    target=n//2
    cnt=int(np.sum(b))
    if cnt>target:
       ones=np.nonzero(b==1.0)[0]
       if len(ones)<cnt: ones=np.arange(n)
       remove=rng.choice(ones, cnt-target, replace=False)
       b[remove]=0.0
    elif cnt<target:
       zeros=np.nonzero(b==0.0)[0]
       add=rng.choice(zeros, target-cnt, replace=False)
       b[add]=1.0
    return b
```
If n odd, target floor? For binary tie need even; skip binary if odd. Here n even.

`self_test_pair_delta` top-level:
```
def self_test_pair_delta():
    global _PAIR_DELTA_OK
    try:
       rng=np.random.RandomState(7)
       n=10
       h=project_feasible(rng.rand(n), n)
       q=np.arange(2*n-1)-(n-1)
       for _ in range(20):
          # choose i,j feasible
          while True:
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
             if i!=j and h[i]>0.4 and h[j]<0.6: break
          a=-0.1; b=0.1
          # ensure h[i]+a>=0 h[j]+b<=1 (true by thresholds)
          corr=compute_corr(h)
          tr=_pair_trial_delta(h,corr,i,j,a,b,q,n)
          h2=h.copy(); h2[i]+=a; h2[j]+=b
          ref=compute_corr(h2)
          if not np.allclose(tr,ref,atol=1e-9,rtol=1e-9):
             _PAIR_DELTA_OK=False; return
       _PAIR_DELTA_OK=True
    except Exception:
       _PAIR_DELTA_OK=False
```
If pair delta path has subtle bug, fallback. Good.

`shift_grad` uses n, kidx. Need if kidx out of range? active top valid.
```
def shift_grad(h,n,kidx):
    q=kidx-(n-1)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if q>=0:
       m=n-q
       if m>0:
          g[q:q+m] += 1.0 - h[:m]
          g[:m] -= h[q:q+m]
    else:
       r=-q; m=n-r
       if m>0:
          g[:m] += 1.0 - h[r:r+m]
          g[r:r+m] -= h[:m]
    return g
```
Check q=0: m=n; g +=1-h; g -=h ->1-2h, good.

`active_grad`:
```
def active_grad(h,n,corr,beta=20.0,top=5):
    if n<=0: return np.zeros(0)
    top_idx=np.argsort(-corr)[:top]
    base=float(corr[top_idx[0]])
    wa=np.exp(beta*(corr[top_idx]-base))
    sw=wa.sum()
    if sw<=0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        wa=np.ones(len(top_idx))/len(top_idx)
    else: wa=wa/sw
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for t in range(len(top_idx)):
       if wa[t] > 1e-12:
          g += float(wa[t]) * shift_grad(h,n,int(top_idx[t]))
    return g
```

`full_dir_improve` needs current corr and maxc to compare? It can compute full corr for trial. Use `q`? no.
```
def full_dir_improve(h, corrt, maxc, g, n):
    d = -g
    d = d - np.mean(d)
    pos=d>1e-12; neg=d<-1e-12
    if not (pos.any() or neg.any()): return None
    amax=1e18
    if pos.any():
       a1=np.min((1.0-h[pos])/d[pos])
       amax=min(amax,float(a1))
    if neg.any():
       a1=np.min(h[neg]/(-d[neg]))
       amax=min(amax,float(a1))
    if not np.isfinite(amax) or amax<=1e-10: return None
    safe=amax*(1.0-1e-10)
    besth=None; bestc=None; bestval=maxc
    fracs=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0]
    for f in fracs:
       aa=safe*float(f)
       h2=h+aa*d
       h2=np.clip(h2,0.0,1.0)
       h2=project_feasible(h2,n)
       c2=compute_corr(h2)
       v=float(np.max(c2))
       if v < bestval - 1e-11:
          bestval=v; besth=h2; bestc=c2
    if besth is None: return None
    return besth,bestc,bestval
```
Maybe `project_feasible` costly but few calls. If d sum zero, projection may not change much. Good.

**SLSQP after pair_descent**: We can call a function `polish_slsqps(h,n, total_time)`:

def slsqp_polish(h,n,betas,time_limit):
   best=h; bestc=compute_c5(best,n)
   start=time.time()
   for beta in betas:
       if time.time()-start > time_limit or remaining()<10: break
       h2=slsqp_refine(best,n,beta,maxiter=150)
       c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < bestc - 1e-13: best=h2; bestc=c2
   return best,bestc
```

`pair_descent` code structure (write carefully):
```
def pair_descent(h0, n, time_limit, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur = float(np.max(corr))
    best_h = h.copy(); best_cur = cur
    q = np.arange(2*n - 1) - (n - 1)
    start = time.time()
    if time_limit is None: time_limit = max(0.0, remaining())
    noimp=0; acc=0; plevel=0
    pars = 8 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 4
    fracs = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0] if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else [0.1,0.5,1.0]
    while remaining() > 12 and (time.time() - start) < time_limit:
        if acc > 400:
            corr = compute_corr(h); cur = float(np.max(corr)); acc=0
        if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
            best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); plevel=0
        g = active_grad(h,n,corr,20.0,5)
        ordered = np.argsort(g)
        improved=False
        # high list
        high_list=[]; high_set=set()
        for idx in ordered[::-1]:
           idx=int(idx)
           if idx not in high_set:
              high_set.add(idx); high_list.append(idx)
              if len(high_list)>=pars: break
        low_list=[]
        for idx in ordered:
           idx=int(idx)
           if idx not in high_set:
              low_list.append(idx)
              if len(low_list)>=pars: break
        for ii in high_list:
           if improved: break
           for pp in low_list:
              if ii==pp: continue
              spread=float(g[ii]-g[pp])
              if spread < 1e-12: continue
              maxd=min(float(h[ii]), 1.0-float(h[pp])) - 1e-11
              if maxd <= 1e-10: continue
              best_tr=None; best_val=cur; best_d=0.0
              for f in fracs:
                 d=float(f)*maxd
                 if d <= 1e-12: continue
                 tr=pair_trial(h,corr,ii,pp,-d,d,q,n)
                 nm=float(np.max(tr))
                 if nm < best_val - 1e-11:
                    best_val=nm; best_tr=tr; best_d=d
              if best_tr is not None:
                 h[ii] -= best_d; h[pp] += best_d
                 corr=best_tr; cur=best_val; acc+=1
                 if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                    best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); plevel=0
                 improved=True; break
        if improved:
            noimp=0
            continue
        # full direction
        res = full_dir_improve(h, corr, cur, g, n)
        if res is not None:
            h2, corr2, cur2 = res
            h=h2; corr=corr2; cur=cur2; acc+=1; noimp=0
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); plevel=0
            continue
        # random pair attempts
        improved=False
        for _ in range(20 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 8):
            ii=int(ordered[rng.randint(n)])
            pp=int(ordered[(rng.randint(n)-1) % n]) # random low? Not useful.
            # Better: ii random high/low? Use sorted random indices: idx1=ordered[rng.randint(n)], idx2=ordered[rng.randint(n)]
            if ii==pp: continue
            maxd=min(float(h[ii]),1.0-float(h[pp])) -1e-11
            if maxd<=1e-10: continue
            best_tr=None; best_val=cur; best_d=0.0
            for f in [0.1,0.3,1.0]:
                d=f*maxd
                if d<=1e-12: continue
                tr=pair_trial(h,corr,ii,pp,-d,d,q,n)
                nm=float(np.max(tr))
                if nm < best_val - 1e-11:
                   best_val=nm; best_tr=tr; best_d=d
            if best_tr is not None:
                h[ii]-=best_d; h[pp]+=best_d; corr=best_tr; cur=best_val; acc+=1; improved=True; ...
                break
        if improved:
            noimp=0; continue
        noimp += 1
        if noimp >= 30:
            sigmas=[0.003,0.008,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
            sigma=sigmas[min(plevel,len(sigmas)-1)]
            plevel = min(plevel+1, len(sigmas)-1)
            h = project_feasible(best_h + sigma*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n), n)
            corr = compute_corr(h); cur=float(np.max(corr)); acc=0
            noimp=0
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h,n)
```
In random pair attempts, using same `ii` decrease and `pp` increase; but if random g order, could be wrong direction (increase high, decrease low). To reduce objective, we should always decrease higher g and increase lower g. For random pairs, choose `ii` from top half and `pp` from bottom half:
```
half=n//2
ii=int(ordered[rng.randint(half, n)])  # higher g? ordered ascending, top half
pp=int(ordered[rng.randint(0, half)])
if g[ii] <= g[pp]: ii,pp=pp,ii
```
Do that. `ordered` from current g before no-improve; still good.

In full_dir accept, `full_dir_improve` returns projected h and corr. If it worsens? It only accepts if improves cur. Good.

After pair accept, `corr=best_tr` which is from delta. If `_PAIR_DELTA_OK` false, full recompute, good.

`acc > 400` recompute; after recompute, cur might differ and `cc`? Fine.

```
def binary_quench(h0,n,time_limit,rng):
    if n%2!=0: return None, inf
    h=to_binary(h0,n,rng)
    corr=compute_corr(h); cur=float(np.max(corr)); best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
    q=np.arange(2*n-1)-(n-1)
    start=time.time()
    noimp=0; acc=0
    tol=max(2.0, 0.005*float(n))
    while remaining()>12 and (time.time()-start)<time_limit:
       if acc>700:
          corr=compute_corr(h); cur=float(np.max(corr)); acc=0
       if cur < best_cur - 1e-9:
          best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
       if cur - best_cur > max(4.0, 0.02*n):
          h=best_h.copy(); corr=compute_corr(h); cur=best_cur; noimp=0; continue
       idx=int(np.argmax(corr)); qq=idx-(n-1)
       ones=np.nonzero(h==1.0)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h==0.0)[0]
       if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
       if qq>=0:
          bad=ones[(ones>=qq)&(h[ones-qq]<0.5)]
          good=zeros[(zeros < n-qq)&(h[zeros+qq]>0.5)]
       else:
          r=-qq
          bad=ones[(ones < n-r)&(h[ones+r]<0.5)]
          good=zeros[(zeros>=r)&(h[zeros-r]>0.5)]
       improved=False
       ntr=120 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 30
       for _ in range(ntr):
          if len(bad)>0 and rng.rand()<0.85: ii=int(bad[rng.randint(len(bad))])
          else: ii=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
          if len(good)>0 and rng.rand()<0.85: pp=int(good[rng.randint(len(good))])
          else: pp=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
          if ii==pp: continue
          if h[ii]<0.5 or h[pp]>0.5: continue
          tr=pair_trial(h,corr,ii,pp,-1.0,1.0,q,n)
          nm=float(np.max(tr))
          if nm < cur - 1e-9:
             h[ii]=0.0; h[pp]=1.0; corr=tr; cur=nm; acc=0
             if cur<best_cur: best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
             improved=True; break
       if improved: noimp=0; continue
       # random all pairs
       for _ in range(20 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 6):
          ii=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); pp=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
          tr=pair_trial(...); nm=...
          if nm < cur-1e-9: accept break
       if improved: noimp=0; continue
       noimp+=1
       if noimp>=30:
          ii=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); pp=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
          tr=pair_trial(...); nm=float(np.max(tr))
          if nm - cur <= tol or rng.rand()<0.03:
             h[ii]=0;h[pp]=1;corr=tr;cur=nm;acc=0
          else:
             h=best_h.copy(); corr=compute_corr(h); cur=best_cur
          noimp=0
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h,n)
```
When random perturb accepts worse, best unchanged. If cur too high, reset. Good.

Need if `bad` or `good` length large, indexing `bad[rng.randint(len(bad))]` okay. If bad empty but ones maybe all? okay.

**SLSQP polish function**:
```
def slsqp_polish(h0,n,betas,time_limit):
    best=project_feasible(h0,n); bestc=compute_c5(best,n)
    start=time.time()
    for beta in betas:
       if remaining()<15 or (time.time()-start)>time_limit: break
       h2=slsqp_refine(best,n,float(beta),maxiter=120)
       c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < bestc - 1e-13:
          best=h2; bestc=c2
    return best,bestc
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_START = 0.0
_BUDGET = 1300.0
_PAIR_DELTA_OK = True

_G_N = 0
_G_BETA = 1.0
_G_TGT = 0.0


def remaining():
    t = time.time() - _START
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET - t - 3.0)


def compute_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    if len(v) != n:
        if len(v) > n:
            v = v[:n]
        else:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            m = min(len(v), n)
            tmp[:m] = v[:m]
            v = tmp

    vm = float(np.min(v))
    vX = float(np.max(v))

    if vm >= -1e-12 and vX <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        w = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-10:
            if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
                idx = int(np.argmax(w * (1.0 - w)))
                nv = w[idx] + diff
                if 0.0 <= nv <= 1.0:
                    w[idx] = nv
            return w

    lo = min(vm, target / float(max(1, n))) - 1.0
    hi = max(vX, target / float(max(1, n))) + 1.0

    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        w = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    w = np.clip(v - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(12):
        w = np.clip(w, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - float(np.sum(w))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        idx = int(np.argmax(w * (1.0 - w)))
        nv = w[idx] + diff
        if 0.0 <= nv <= 1.0:
            w[idx] = nv
        else:
            mask = (w > 1e-12) & (w < 1.0 - 1e-12)
            cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                w[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            else:
                w += diff / float(n)

    return np.clip(w, 0.0, 1.0)


def interp_to(src, dst):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    sn = len(src)
    if dst == sn:
        return src.copy()
    xp = (np.arange(sn, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(sn))
    fx = (np.arange(dst, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(dst))
    return np.interp(fx, xp, src)


def to_binary(h, n, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    b = np.where(h > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).copy()
    target = n // 2
    cnt = int(np.sum(b))
    if cnt > target:
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1.0)[0]
        if len(ones) >= cnt:
            rem = rng.choice(ones, cnt - target, replace=False)
            b[rem] = 0.0
        else:
            b[:] = 0.0
            idx = rng.choice(n, target, replace=False)
            b[idx] = 1.0
    elif cnt < target:
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0.0)[0]
        if len(zeros) >= target - cnt:
            add = rng.choice(zeros, target - cnt, replace=False)
            b[add] = 1.0
        else:
            b[:] = 1.0
            idx = rng.choice(n, n - target, replace=False)
            b[idx] = 0.0
    return b


def _pair_trial_delta(h, corr, i, j, a, b, q, n):
    tr = corr.copy()
    L = len(q)
    center = n - 1

    mi = (q >= (i - n + 1)) & (q <= i)
    if mi.any():
        dst = i - q[mi]
        tr[mi] += a - a * h[dst]

    pi = (q >= -i) & (q < (n - i))
    if pi.any():
        tr[pi] += -a * h[i + q[pi]]

    mj = (q >= (j - n + 1)) & (q <= j)
    if mj.any():
        dst = j - q[mj]
        tr[mj] += b - b * h[dst]

    pj = (q >= -j) & (q < (n - j))
    if pj.any():
        tr[pj] += -b * h[j + q[pj]]

    tr[center] -= a * a + b * b

    d = i - j
    idx1 = center + d
    idx2 = center - d
    if 0 <= idx1 < L:
        tr[idx1] += -a * b
    if 0 <= idx2 < L:
        tr[idx2] += -a * b

    return tr


def pair_trial(h, corr, i, j, a, b, q, n):
    if _PAIR_DELTA_OK:
        return _pair_trial_delta(h, corr, i, j, a, b, q, n)
    h2 = h.copy()
    h2[i] += a
    h2[j] += b
    h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
    return compute_corr(h2)


def self_test_pair_delta():
    global _PAIR_DELTA_OK
    try:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(77)
        n = 10
        h = project_feasible(rng.rand(n), n)
        q = np.arange(2 * n - 1) - (n - 1)
        for _ in range(25):
            for _2 in range(50):
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if ii != jj and h[ii] > 0.35 and h[jj] < 0.65:
                    break
            else:
                continue
            a = -0.08
            b = 0.08
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            tr = _pair_trial_delta(h, corr, ii, jj, a, b, q, n)
            h2 = h.copy()
            h2[ii] += a
            h2[jj] += b
            ref = compute_corr(h2)
            if not np.allclose(tr, ref, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-9):
                _PAIR_DELTA_OK = False
                return
        _PAIR_DELTA_OK = True
    except Exception:
        _PAIR_DELTA_OK = False


def shift_grad(h, n, kidx):
    q = int(kidx) - (n - 1)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if q >= 0:
        m = n - q
        if m > 0:
            g[q:q + m] += 1.0 - h[:m]
            g[:m] -= h[q:q + m]
    else:
        r = -q
        m = n - r
        if m > 0:
            g[:m] += 1.0 - h[r:r + m]
            g[r:r + m] -= h[:m]
    return g


def active_grad(h, n, corr, beta=20.0, top=5):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    top_idx = np.argsort(-corr)[:top]
    base = float(corr[top_idx[0]])
    wa = np.exp(beta * (corr[top_idx] - base))
    sw = float(np.sum(wa))
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        wa = np.ones(len(top_idx), dtype=float) / float(len(top_idx))
    else:
        wa = wa / sw

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for t in range(len(top_idx)):
        w = float(wa[t])
        if w > 1e-12:
            g += w * shift_grad(h, n, int(top_idx[t]))
    return g


def full_soft_obj(x):
    n = _G_N
    beta = _G_BETA
    corr = compute_corr(x)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    s = beta * (corr - m)
    s = np.clip(s, -700.0, 700.0)
    ex = np.exp(s)
    return (m + float(np.log(float(np.sum(ex)))) / beta) * (2.0 / float(n))


def full_soft_grad(x):
    n = _G_N
    beta = _G_BETA
    corr = compute_corr(x)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    s = np.clip(beta * (corr - m), -700.0, 700.0)
    ex = np.exp(s)
    w = ex / float(np.sum(ex))

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    c0 = n - 1
    w0 = float(w[c0])
    if w0 > 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)

    center = n - 1
    for q in range(1, n):
        wp = float(w[center + q])
        wm = float(w[center - q])
        s = wp + wm
        if s <= 1e-15:
            continue
        a = n - q
        g[:a] += wm - s * x[q:q + a]
        g[q:] += wp - s * x[:a]

    return g * (2.0 / float(n))


def _sum_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x)) - _G_TGT


def _sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_G_N, dtype=float)


def slsqp_refine(h0, n, beta, maxiter=140):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA, _G_TGT
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    _G_N = int(n)
    _G_BETA = float(beta)
    _G_TGT = 0.5 * float(n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sum_fun, "jac": _sum_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            full_soft_obj,
            h0,
            jac=full_soft_grad,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-13, "iprint": 0},
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return h0


def slsqp_polish(h0, n, betas, time_limit):
    best = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best, n)
    start = time.time()

    for beta in betas:
        if remaining() < 12.0:
            break
        if (time.time() - start) > max(1.0, float(time_limit)):
            break
        h2 = slsqp_refine(best, n, float(beta), maxiter=120)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
            best = h2
            best_c = c2

    return best, best_c


def full_dir_improve(h, corr, maxc, g, n):
    d = -g
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    pos = d > 1e-12
    neg = d < -1e-12
    if not (pos.any() or neg.any()):
        return None

    a_max = 1e18
    if pos.any():
        val = np.min((1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos])
        if np.isfinite(val):
            a_max = min(a_max, float(val))
    if neg.any():
        val = np.min(h[neg] / (-d[neg]))
        if np.isfinite(val):
            a_max = min(a_max, float(val))

    if not np.isfinite(a_max) or a_max <= 1e-11:
        return None

    safe = a_max * (1.0 - 1e-10)
    best_h = None
    best_c = None
    best_v = maxc

    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.00)
    for f in fracs:
        aa = safe * float(f)
        h2 = h + aa * d
        h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        h2 = project_feasible(h2, n)
        c2 = compute_corr(h2)
        v2 = float(np.max(c2))
        if v2 < best_v - 1e-10:
            best_v = v2
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if best_h is None:
        return None
    return best_h, best_c, best_v


def pair_descent(h0, n, time_limit, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur = float(np.max(corr))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cur = cur

    q = np.arange(2 * n - 1) - (n - 1)
    start = time.time()
    if time_limit is None:
        time_limit = max(0.0, remaining())

    noimp = 0
    acc = 0
    plevel = 0

    pars = 8 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 4
    if _PAIR_DELTA_OK:
        fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.00)
    else:
        fracs = (0.10, 0.50, 1.00)

    while remaining() > 12.0 and (time.time() - start) < float(time_limit):
        if acc > 400:
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            cur = float(np.max(corr))
            acc = 0

        if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
            best_cur = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
            plevel = 0

        g = active_grad(h, n, corr, 20.0, 5)
        ordered = np.argsort(g)

        improved = False

        high_list = []
        high_set = set()
        for idx in ordered[::-1]:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in high_set:
                high_set.add(idx)
                high_list.append(idx)
                if len(high_list) >= pars:
                    break

        low_list = []
        for idx in ordered:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in high_set:
                low_list.append(idx)
                if len(low_list) >= pars:
                    break

        for ii in high_list:
            if improved:
                break
            gi = float(g[ii])
            hi_val = float(h[ii])
            if hi_val <= 1e-11:
                continue
            for pp in low_list:
                if ii == pp:
                    continue
                gp = float(g[pp])
                valp = float(h[pp])
                spread = gi - gp
                if spread < 1e-11:
                    continue
                room = 1.0 - valp
                if room <= 1e-11:
                    continue
                maxd = min(hi_val, room) - 1e-11
                if maxd <= 1e-10:
                    continue

                best_tr = None
                best_val = cur
                best_d = 0.0
                for f in fracs:
                    dd = float(f) * maxd
                    if dd <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    tr = pair_trial(h, corr, ii, pp, -dd, dd, q, n)
                    nm = float(np.max(tr))
                    if nm < best_val - 1e-10:
                        best_val = nm
                        best_tr = tr
                        best_d = dd

                if best_tr is not None:
                    h[ii] -= best_d
                    h[pp] += best_d
                    corr = best_tr
                    cur = best_val
                    acc += 1
                    if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                        best_cur = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        plevel = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            continue

        res = full_dir_improve(h, corr, cur, g, n)
        if res is not None:
            h2, corr2, cur2 = res
            h = h2
            corr = corr2
            cur = cur2
            acc += 1
            noimp = 0
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                best_cur = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
                plevel = 0
            continue

        improved = False
        half = max(1, n // 2)
        rand_fracs = (0.10, 0.30, 1.00)
        for _ in range(20 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 8):
            ii = int(ordered[rng.randint(half, n)])
            pp = int(ordered[rng.randint(0, half)])
            if ii == pp:
                continue
            if float(g[ii]) <= float(g[pp]):
                ii, pp = pp, ii
            maxd = min(float(h[ii]), 1.0 - float(h[pp])) - 1e-11
            if maxd <= 1e-10:
                continue
            best_tr = None
            best_val = cur
            best_d = 0.0
            for f in rand_fracs:
                dd = float(f) * maxd
                if dd <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                tr = pair_trial(h, corr, ii, pp, -dd, dd, q, n)
                nm = float(np.max(tr))
                if nm < best_val - 1e-10:
                    best_val = nm
                    best_tr = tr
                    best_d = dd
            if best_tr is not None:
                h[ii] -= best_d
                h[pp] += best_d
                corr = best_tr
                cur = best_val
                acc += 1
                improved = True
                if cur < best_cur - 1e-13:
                    best_cur = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                break

        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            continue

        noimp += 1
        if noimp >= 30:
            sigmas = (0.003, 0.008, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20)
            sigma = sigmas[min(plevel, len(sigmas) - 1)]
            plevel = min(plevel + 1, len(sigmas) - 1)
            h = project_feasible(best_h + sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n), n)
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            cur = float(np.max(corr))
            acc = 0
            noimp = 0

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h, n)


def binary_quench(h0, n, time_limit, rng):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    h = to_binary(h0, n, rng)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    cur = float(np.max(corr))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cur = cur

    q = np.arange(2 * n - 1) - (n - 1)
    start = time.time()
    if time_limit is None:
        time_limit = max(0.0, remaining())

    noimp = 0
    acc = 0
    tol = max(2.0, 0.005 * float(n))

    while remaining() > 12.0 and (time.time() - start) < float(time_limit):
        if acc > 800:
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            cur = float(np.max(corr))
            acc = 0

        if cur < best_cur - 1e-9:
            best_cur = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        if cur - best_cur > max(4.0, 0.02 * float(n)):
            h = best_h.copy()
            corr = compute_corr(h)
            cur = best_cur
            noimp = 0
            continue

        idx = int(np.argmax(corr))
        qq = idx - (n - 1)

        ones = np.nonzero(h == 1.0)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0.0)[0]
        if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
            break

        if qq >= 0:
            bad = ones[(ones >= qq) & (h[ones - qq] < 0.5)]
            good = zeros[(zeros < (n - qq)) & (h[zeros + qq] > 0.5)]
        else:
            r = -qq
            bad = ones[(ones < (n - r)) & (h[ones + r] < 0.5)]
            good = zeros[(zeros >= r) & (h[zeros - r] > 0.5)]

        improved = False
        ntry = 120 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 30
        for _ in range(ntry):
            if len(bad) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.85:
                ii = int(bad[rng.randint(len(bad))])
            else:
                ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            if len(good) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.85:
                pp = int(good[rng.randint(len(good))])
            else:
                pp = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])

            if ii == pp:
                continue
            if h[ii] < 0.5 or h[pp] > 0.5:
                continue

            tr = pair_trial(h, corr, ii, pp, -1.0, 1.0, q, n)
            nm = float(np.max(tr))
            if nm < cur - 1e-9:
                h[ii] = 0.0
                h[pp] = 1.0
                corr = tr
                cur = nm
                acc = 0
                if cur < best_cur:
                    best_cur = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            continue

        for _ in range(20 if _PAIR_DELTA_OK else 6):
            ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            pp = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            if ii == pp:
                continue
            tr = pair_trial(h, corr, ii, pp, -1.0, 1.0, q, n)
            nm = float(np.max(tr))
            if nm < cur - 1e-9:
                h[ii] = 0.0
                h[pp] = 1.0
                corr = tr
                cur = nm
                acc = 0
                improved = True
                if cur < best_cur:
                    best_cur = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                break

        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            continue

        noimp += 1
        if noimp >= 30:
            ii = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            pp = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            tr = pair_trial(h, corr, ii, pp, -1.0, 1.0, q, n)
            nm = float(np.max(tr))
            if (nm - cur) <= tol or rng.rand() < 0.03:
                h[ii] = 0.0
                h[pp] = 1.0
                corr = tr
                cur = nm
                acc = 0
            else:
                h = best_h.copy()
                corr = compute_corr(h)
                cur = best_cur
            noimp = 0

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _START, _BUDGET

    _START = time.time()
    _BUDGET = float(budget_s)

    try:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(42)

    self_test_pair_delta()

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        pn = len(parent)
        if pn > 0:
            ph = project_feasible(parent, pn)
            pc = compute_c5(ph, pn)
            if np.isfinite(pc):
                best_h = ph
                best_c = pc
                best_n = pn
            try:
                ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(ph)
                ev = float(ev)
                if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_c:
                    best_c = ev
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 320
        hrand = np.random.RandomState(12345).rand(best_n)
        ker = np.ones(11, dtype=float) / 11.0
        hsmooth = np.convolve(hrand, ker, mode="same")
        best_h = project_feasible(hsmooth, best_n)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if best_n % 2 != 0:
        nn = best_n - 1
        if nn < 128:
            nn = best_n + 1
        best_h = project_feasible(interp_to(best_h, best_n, nn), nn)
        best_n = nn
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    base_n = best_n
    cands = [base_n]
    for off in (16, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 160, 192):
        cands.append(base_n + off)
    for off in (16, 32, 48):
        cands.append(base_n - off)
    cands.extend([384, 512, 640])

    res_list = []
    seen = set()
    for nn in cands:
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            continue
        if nn < 128 or nn > 720:
            continue
        if nn not in seen:
            seen.add(nn)
            res_list.append(nn)

    if not res_list:
        res_list = [base_n if base_n % 2 == 0 else base_n + 1]

    res_list = res_list[:9]

    base_times = [260.0, 130.0, 105.0, 85.0, 75.0, 65.0, 55.0, 45.0, 35.0]

    for idx, nn in enumerate(res_list):
        if remaining() < 40.0:
            break
        alloc = min(base_times[idx], remaining() * 0.40)
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break

        if nn == best_n:
            h0 = best_h.copy()
        else:
            h0 = interp_to(best_h, best_n, nn)
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, nn)

        cand_h = h0
        cand_c = compute_c5(cand_h, nn)

        pd_time = min(alloc * 0.55, 220.0 if idx == 0 else 95.0)
        if pd_time > 15.0 and remaining() > pd_time + 10.0:
            h1, c1 = pair_descent(cand_h, nn, pd_time, rng)
            if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < cand_c:
                cand_h = h1
                cand_c = c1

        if remaining() > 25.0 and nn <= 480 and idx <= 4:
            sp_time = min(alloc * 0.25, 60.0 if idx == 0 else 30.0)
            if idx == 0:
                betas = (200.0, 900.0, 3500.0, 9000.0)
            else:
                betas = (400.0, 1600.0, 5000.0)
            h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(cand_h, nn, betas, sp_time)
            if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < cand_c:
                cand_h = h2
                cand_c = c2

        if remaining() > 20.0 and (nn % 2 == 0) and nn <= 512:
            bq_time = min(alloc * 0.20, 35.0 if idx == 0 else 15.0)
            h3, c3 = binary_quench(cand_h, nn, bq_time, rng)
            if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < cand_c:
                cand_h = h3
                cand_c = c3

        if np.isfinite(cand_c) and cand_c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = cand_c
            best_h = cand_h
            best_n = nn

    if remaining() > 60.0:
        pd_time = min(150.0, remaining() * 0.35)
        h1, c1 = pair_descent(best_h, best_n, pd_time, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1

    if remaining() > 45.0 and best_n <= 480:
        h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, (1000.0, 4000.0, 12000.0), min(80.0, remaining() * 0.30))
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

    if remaining() > 30.0 and (best_n % 2 == 0) and best_n <= 512:
        h3, c3 = binary_quench(best_h, best_n, min(40.0, remaining() * 0.25), rng)
        if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_h = h3

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        ev = float(ev)
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_c:
            best_c = ev
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n))
```
